+"""SOURCE ONLY. Finite metadata readiness and failure-only absent-leaf probe.
+No archive bytes are opened. This does not grant execution or hostile-code safety.
+Full isolated startup needs an independently observed receipt before owner use.
+"""
+import errno, hashlib, json, os, pathlib, re, stat, sys
+
+ARCHIVE = '<PROJECT>/.runtime/hermes-telegram-test/tools/python-3.14.7+20260901-linux-x64/lib/python314.zip'
+INTERPRETER = '<PROJECT>/.runtime/hermes-telegram-test/env/bin/python'
+_open, _close, _stat, _fstat = os.open, os.close, os.stat, os.fstat
+
+
+class ProbeRefusal(RuntimeError):
+    pass
+
+
+def require(ok, reason):
+    if not ok:
+        raise ProbeRefusal(reason)
+
+
+def identity(s):
+    return [s.st_dev, s.st_ino, s.st_mode, s.st_uid, s.st_gid]
+
+
+def load(source):
+    m = json.loads((pathlib.Path(source) / 'import-path-pins.json').read_bytes())
+    require(m.get('schema') == 1 and m.get('declared_absent_path') == ARCHIVE and
+            m.get('interpreter', {}).get('path') == INTERPRETER and
+            m.get('runtime_read_authority_expanded') is False, 'ABSENT_DECLARATION_MISMATCH')
+    parent = pathlib.Path(ARCHIVE).parent
+    expected = list(reversed(parent.parents)) + [parent]
+    require([a['path'] for a in m['ancestors']] == [str(p) for p in expected] and
+            all(len(a['identity']) == 5 and all(type(v) is int for v in a['identity']) and
+                stat.S_ISDIR(a['identity'][2]) for a in m['ancestors']), 'EXACT_ANCESTOR_CHAIN_REQUIRED')
+    expected_startup = [ARCHIVE, str(parent / 'python3.14'), str(parent / 'python3.14/lib-dynload'),
+                        str(pathlib.Path(INTERPRETER).parent.parent / 'lib/python3.14/site-packages')]
+    require(m['startup_expected'] == expected_startup and
+            m['interpreter']['sha256'] == '8dfa9757a52b9c3edf1dedaaa2a7a8c40ea4beb058f20e90bdd47b48f3b1b176',
+            'FINITE_STARTUP_DECLARATION_REQUIRED')
+    site = pathlib.Path(expected_startup[-1]); stdlib = pathlib.Path(expected_startup[1])
+    require(set(m['startup_files']) == {str(stdlib / 'site.py'), str(pathlib.Path(INTERPRETER).parent.parent / 'pyvenv.cfg'),
+                str(site / '_virtualenv.pth'), str(site / 'distutils-precedence.pth'),
+                str(site / '_virtualenv.py'), str(site / '_distutils_hack/__init__.py')} and
+            set(m['causal_files']) == {str(stdlib / 'importlib/metadata/__init__.py'), str(stdlib / 'zipfile/__init__.py'),
+                str(stdlib / 'zipfile/_path/__init__.py'), str(site / 'pydantic/plugin/_loader.py'),
+                str(site / 'pydantic/plugin/_schema_validator.py')} and
+            m['pth_inventory'] == ['_virtualenv.pth', 'distutils-precedence.pth'], 'FULL_STARTUP_CAUSAL_PINS_REQUIRED')
+    require(set(m['directory_identities']) == set(m['metadata_inventories']) and
+            all(raw in m['directory_identities'] for raw in expected_startup[1:]), 'FINITE_METADATA_ROOTS_REQUIRED')
+    return m
+
+
+def absent_leaf(m):
+    """Always raises: SAME kernel final-leaf ENOENT, or an explicit refusal.
+    O_PATH|DIRECTORY|NOFOLLOW descriptors reach each exact ancestor; only final
+    no-follow stat can produce the approved missing-leaf exception. Never stat
+    then return permission. Even appearance after stat cannot open content.
+    """
+    handles = []
+    try:
+        for index, row in enumerate(m['ancestors']):
+            path = row['path']
+            try:
+                # CPython's open audit event omits dir_fd. Keep the descriptor
+                # anchor in the absolute /proc path so the unchanged guard can
+                # check the real ancestor instead of an unrelated cwd/name.
+                reached = '/' if index == 0 else '/proc/self/fd/' + str(handles[-1]) + '/' + pathlib.Path(path).name
+                fd = _open(reached, os.O_PATH | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC)
+                handles.append(fd)
+                require(identity(_fstat(fd)) == row['identity'], 'ABSENT_ANCESTOR_IDENTITY_DRIFT')
+            except OSError as error:
+                raise ProbeRefusal('ABSENT_ANCESTOR_ERROR:' + str(error.errno)) from error
+        try:
+            _stat(pathlib.Path(m['declared_absent_path']).name,
+                  dir_fd=handles[-1], follow_symlinks=False)
+        except FileNotFoundError as error:
+            require(error.errno == errno.ENOENT and error.filename == pathlib.Path(ARCHIVE).name and
+                    error.filename2 is None, 'NOT_GENUINE_FINAL_LEAF_ENOENT')
+            # Keep each parent->child binding current, including rename/replacement
+            # while an O_PATH descriptor still reaches the former directory.
+            for index, row in enumerate(m['ancestors']):
+                try:
+                    current = _stat('/' if index == 0 else pathlib.Path(row['path']).name,
+                                    **({} if index == 0 else {'dir_fd': handles[index - 1]}),
+                                    follow_symlinks=False)
+                    require(identity(current) == row['identity'] and
+                            identity(_fstat(handles[index])) == row['identity'],
+                            'ABSENT_ANCESTOR_BINDING_DRIFT')
+                except OSError as changed:
+                    raise ProbeRefusal('ABSENT_ANCESTOR_RECHECK_ERROR:' + str(changed.errno)) from changed
+            # Bare raise preserves the exact kernel exception; no original open.
+            raise
+        except OSError as error:
+            raise ProbeRefusal('ABSENT_LEAF_UNKNOWN:' + str(error.errno)) from error
+        raise ProbeRefusal('DECLARED_ABSENT_LEAF_PRESENT')
+    finally:
+        for fd in reversed(handles):
+            _close(fd)
+
+
+def failure_only_open(m, path, flags, canonical):
+    if isinstance(path, int):
+        require(canonical != ARCHIVE, 'DECLARED_LEAF_DESCRIPTOR_REFUSED')
+        return
+    raw = os.fsdecode(path)
+    lexical = os.path.abspath(raw)
+    proc = re.fullmatch(r'/proc/self/fd/([0-9]+)/([^/]+)', lexical)
+    anchored = False
+    if proc and proc[2] == pathlib.Path(ARCHIVE).name:
+        fd = int(proc[1])
+        anchored = (os.readlink('/proc/self/fd/' + str(fd)) == str(pathlib.Path(ARCHIVE).parent) and
+                    identity(_fstat(fd)) == m['ancestors'][-1]['identity'])
+    # Check exact lexical name BEFORE canonical symlink resolution can hide a
+    # present/dangling leaf. Canonical aliases are refused, never generalized.
+    if lexical != ARCHIVE and canonical != ARCHIVE and not anchored:
+        return
+    require(lexical == ARCHIVE or anchored, 'DECLARED_LEAF_ALIAS_REFUSED')
+    require(not flags & (os.O_WRONLY | os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC | os.O_APPEND),
+            'DECLARED_LEAF_WRITE_REFUSED')
+    absent_leaf(m)  # Cannot return; original content-open is unreachable.
+
+
+def verify_snapshot(m):
+    try:
+        absent_leaf(m)
+    except FileNotFoundError as error:
+        require(error.errno == errno.ENOENT, 'ABSENCE_NOT_ENOENT')
+    for raw, expected in m['startup_files'].items() | m['causal_files'].items():
+        p = pathlib.Path(raw)
+        require(not p.is_symlink() and hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() == expected,
+                'STARTUP_OR_CAUSAL_SOURCE_DRIFT:' + raw)
+    for raw, expected in m['directory_identities'].items():
+        p = pathlib.Path(raw)
+        require(not p.is_symlink() and identity(p.lstat()) == expected and
+                os.access(p, os.R_OK | os.X_OK), 'SEARCH_DIRECTORY_DRIFT:' + raw)
+        # Actually enumerate, so permission/ENOTDIR is not merely predicted.
+        names = sorted(os.listdir(p))
+        found = [n for n in names if n.endswith(('.dist-info', '.egg-info'))]
+        require(found == sorted(m['metadata_inventories'][raw]), 'METADATA_INVENTORY_DRIFT:' + raw)
+        for name, item in m['metadata_inventories'][raw].items():
+            root = p / name
+            require(not root.is_symlink() and identity(root.lstat()) == item['identity'],
+                    'METADATA_IDENTITY_DRIFT:' + str(root))
+            files = [root] if root.is_file() else sorted(root.rglob('*'))
+            require(not any(f.is_symlink() for f in files), 'METADATA_SYMLINK')
+            require({str(f) for f in files if f.is_file()} == set(item['files']), 'METADATA_FILE_INVENTORY_DRIFT')
+            for file, digest in item['files'].items():
+                require(hashlib.sha256(pathlib.Path(file).read_bytes()).hexdigest() == digest,
+                        'METADATA_FILE_DRIFT:' + file)
+    site = pathlib.Path(m['startup_expected'][-1])
+    require(sorted(p.name for p in site.glob('*.pth')) == m['pth_inventory'], 'PTH_INVENTORY_DRIFT')
+    for raw in (m['startup_expected'][1], m['startup_expected'][-1]):
+        for name in ('sitecustomize.py', 'usercustomize.py', 'sitecustomize', 'usercustomize'):
+            p = pathlib.Path(raw) / name
+            require(not p.exists() and not p.is_symlink(), 'UNREVIEWED_CUSTOMIZE')
+    require(m['pydantic_entrypoints'] == [], 'NEW_PYDANTIC_IMPLEMENTATION_NEEDS_REVIEW')
+
+
+def verify_startup(m):
+    require(sys.executable == INTERPRETER and sys.flags.isolated == 1 and
+            sys.flags.no_site == 0 and sys.flags.no_user_site == 1 and
+            sys.flags.dont_write_bytecode == 1 and list(sys.path) == m['startup_expected'],
+            'ACTUAL_ISOLATED_STARTUP_PATH_MISMATCH')
+    for name, key in [('_virtualenv', '_virtualenv.py'), ('_distutils_hack', '_distutils_hack/__init__.py')]:
+        module = sys.modules.get(name)
+        path = getattr(module, '__file__', None)
+        require(path in m['startup_files'] and path.endswith(key), 'ACTUAL_PTH_SHIM_MISSING_OR_FOREIGN')
+
+
+def verify_paths(m, paths, mode, source, run, project, native, initial=False):
+    prefix = [str(source)] if mode == '@canary' else [str(project / 'tests'), str(native), str(project), str(run)]
+    expected = prefix + m['startup_expected']
+    if initial:
+        require(list(paths) == expected, 'POST_PREPEND_PATH_MISMATCH')
+    else:
+        require(list(paths)[-4:] == m['startup_expected'], 'STARTUP_PATH_REMOVED_OR_REORDERED')
+        allowed = set(m['directory_identities']) | {str(source), str(run)}
+        require(all(type(p) is str and (p == ARCHIVE or p in allowed) for p in paths),
+                'UNDECLARED_PYTEST_SEARCH_ENTRY')
+    for raw in paths:
+        if raw == ARCHIVE:
+            try:
+                absent_leaf(m)
+            except FileNotFoundError:
+                pass
+        else:
+            p = pathlib.Path(raw)
+            require(not p.is_symlink() and p.is_dir() and os.access(p, os.R_OK | os.X_OK),
+                    'SEARCH_ENTRY_NOT_ENUMERABLE_DIRECTORY')
+            if raw in m['directory_identities']:
+                require(identity(p.lstat()) == m['directory_identities'][raw], 'SEARCH_ENTRY_IDENTITY_DRIFT')
+
+
+def verify_owner_startup_receipt(source, path, digest):
+    p = pathlib.Path(path)
+    require(p.is_absolute() and not p.is_symlink() and p.resolve(strict=True) == p,
+            'EXACT_STARTUP_RECEIPT_REQUIRED')
+    st = p.lstat()
+    require(stat.S_ISREG(st.st_mode) and st.st_uid == os.getuid() and st.st_nlink == 1 and
+            stat.S_IMODE(st.st_mode) == 0o400, 'IMMUTABLE_STARTUP_RECEIPT_REQUIRED')
+    b = p.read_bytes()
+    require(hashlib.sha256(b).hexdigest() == digest, 'STARTUP_RECEIPT_HASH_DRIFT')
+    r = json.loads(b); m = load(source)
+    require(r.get('state') == 'INDEPENDENT_FULL_ISOLATED_STARTUP_OBSERVED' and
+            r.get('interpreter_sha256') == m['interpreter']['sha256'] and
+            r.get('import_path_pins_sha256') == hashlib.sha256((pathlib.Path(source) / 'import-path-pins.json').read_bytes()).hexdigest() and
+            r.get('sys_path') == m['startup_expected'] and
+            r.get('boot_id') == pathlib.Path('/proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id').read_text().strip() and
+            r.get('flags') == {'isolated': 1, 'no_site': 0, 'no_user_site': 1, 'dont_write_bytecode': 1} and
+            r.get('actual_shims') == {name: next(k for k in m['startup_files'] if k.endswith(suffix)) for name, suffix in
+                                    [('_virtualenv', '_virtualenv.py'), ('_distutils_hack', '_distutils_hack/__init__.py')]} and
+            r.get('candidate_imported') is False and r.get('backend_terminal_reaped') is True,
+            'FULL_STARTUP_OBSERVATION_NOT_EVIDENCED')
+    return {'path': str(p), 'sha256': digest}

+"""SOURCE ONLY, NOT_RUN. Standalone changed-area controls, never production grants.
+The synthetic declaration is confined to pytest's private tmp_path. This module
+does not admit a guard/collection or alter the installed archive/interpreter.
+Future execution requires separate exact source review and owner admission.
+"""
+import errno, importlib.util, os, pathlib, stat
+import pytest
+
+
+def module():
+    path = pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name('import_path_readiness.py')
+    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location('isolated_import_path_source_test', path)
+    obj = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
+    spec.loader.exec_module(obj)
+    return obj
+
+
+def fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    api = module()
+    parent = tmp_path / 'pinned-parent'
+    parent.mkdir()
+    leaf = parent / 'python314.zip'
+    chain = list(reversed(parent.parents)) + [parent]
+    m = {'declared_absent_path': str(leaf),
+         'ancestors': [{'path': str(p), 'identity': api.identity(p.lstat())} for p in chain]}
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, 'ARCHIVE', str(leaf))
+    return api, m, leaf
+
+
+def test_same_genuine_kernel_enoent_and_no_content_open(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    actual_stat, actual_open = api._stat, api._open
+    saved = []
+    flags_seen = []
+    def metadata_stat(*args, **kwargs):
+        try:
+            return actual_stat(*args, **kwargs)
+        except FileNotFoundError as error:
+            saved.append(error)
+            raise
+    def metadata_open(*args, **kwargs):
+        flags_seen.append(args[1])
+        assert args[1] == os.O_PATH | os.O_DIRECTORY | os.O_NOFOLLOW | os.O_CLOEXEC
+        return actual_open(*args, **kwargs)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, '_stat', metadata_stat)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, '_open', metadata_open)
+    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError) as result:
+        api.failure_only_open(m, str(leaf), os.O_RDONLY, str(leaf))
+    assert result.value is saved[0]
+    assert result.value.errno == errno.ENOENT
+    assert result.value.filename == leaf.name
+    assert len(flags_seen) == len(m['ancestors'])
+    assert not leaf.exists()
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind', ['file', 'directory', 'link', 'dangling', 'fifo'])
+def test_any_existing_leaf_refuses_without_open(tmp_path, monkeypatch, kind):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    if kind == 'file': leaf.write_bytes(b'unreviewed bytes')
+    elif kind == 'directory': leaf.mkdir()
+    elif kind == 'fifo': os.mkfifo(leaf)
+    elif kind == 'link': leaf.symlink_to(tmp_path)
+    else: leaf.symlink_to(tmp_path / 'missing')
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='DECLARED_ABSENT_LEAF_PRESENT'):
+        api.failure_only_open(m, str(leaf), os.O_RDONLY, os.path.realpath(leaf))
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('flags', [os.O_WRONLY, os.O_RDWR, os.O_CREAT,
+                                  os.O_TRUNC, os.O_APPEND, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT])
+def test_every_write_route_refuses_before_metadata(tmp_path, monkeypatch, flags):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, '_open', lambda *a, **k: pytest.fail('metadata dispatched after write refusal'))
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='DECLARED_LEAF_WRITE_REFUSED'):
+        api.failure_only_open(m, str(leaf), flags, str(leaf))
+
+
+def test_unknown_missing_sibling_does_not_get_absence_route(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, '_open', lambda *a, **k: pytest.fail('unknown path got exception route'))
+    other = leaf.with_name('python313.zip')
+    assert api.failure_only_open(m, str(other), os.O_RDONLY, str(other)) is None
+    # None is ordinary existing guard continuation; its unchanged readable()
+    # and hard-denial routes decide unknown foreign missing paths, not this API.
+
+
+def test_directory_identity_drift_refuses(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    m['ancestors'][-1]['identity'][1] += 1
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='IDENTITY_DRIFT'):
+        api.absent_leaf(m)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind', ['missing', 'symlink', 'file'])
+def test_ancestor_missing_symlink_enotdir_are_not_leaf_enoent(tmp_path, monkeypatch, kind):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    leaf.parent.rmdir()
+    if kind == 'symlink': leaf.parent.symlink_to(tmp_path)
+    elif kind == 'file': leaf.parent.write_bytes(b'not directory')
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='ABSENT_ANCESTOR_ERROR'):
+        api.absent_leaf(m)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize('number', [errno.EACCES, errno.ENOTDIR, errno.ELOOP, errno.EIO])
+def test_unknown_leaf_errors_are_not_fabricated_enoent(tmp_path, monkeypatch, number):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    def failed_stat(*args, **kwargs):
+        raise OSError(number, 'synthetic syscall error', leaf.name)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, '_stat', failed_stat)
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='ABSENT_LEAF_UNKNOWN'):
+        api.absent_leaf(m)
+
+
+def test_appearance_after_metadata_preserves_same_error_and_next_refuses(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    real_stat = api._stat
+    saved = []
+    def after_absence(*args, **kwargs):
+        try:
+            return real_stat(*args, **kwargs)
+        except FileNotFoundError as error:
+            saved.append(error)
+            leaf.write_bytes(b'appeared after kernel negative observation')
+            raise
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, '_stat', after_absence)
+    with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError) as result:
+        api.failure_only_open(m, str(leaf), os.O_RDONLY, str(leaf))
+    assert result.value is saved[0]
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, '_stat', real_stat)
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='DECLARED_ABSENT_LEAF_PRESENT'):
+        api.failure_only_open(m, str(leaf), os.O_RDONLY, str(leaf))
+
+
+def test_exact_directory_descriptor_anchor(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    fd = os.open(leaf.parent, os.O_PATH | os.O_DIRECTORY)
+    try:
+        with pytest.raises(FileNotFoundError):
+            api.failure_only_open(m, '/proc/self/fd/' + str(fd) + '/' + leaf.name,
+                                  os.O_RDONLY, str(leaf))
+    finally:
+        os.close(fd)
+

+def test_alias_and_existing_descriptor_refused(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    alias = tmp_path / 'alias'
+    alias.symlink_to(leaf)
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='ALIAS_REFUSED'):
+        api.failure_only_open(m, str(alias), os.O_RDONLY, str(leaf))
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='DESCRIPTOR_REFUSED'):
+        api.failure_only_open(m, 99, os.O_RDONLY, str(leaf))

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('entry', ['missing', 'regular', 'symlink'])
+def test_additional_search_entry_not_automatically_admitted(tmp_path, entry):
+    api = module()
+    foreign = tmp_path / entry
+    if entry == 'regular': foreign.write_bytes(b'no archive permission')
+    elif entry == 'symlink': foreign.symlink_to(tmp_path)
+    m = {'startup_expected': [api.ARCHIVE, '/stdlib', '/dynload', '/site'],
+         'directory_identities': {}}
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='UNDECLARED'):
+        api.verify_paths(m, [str(foreign)] + m['startup_expected'], '@canary',
+                         tmp_path, tmp_path, tmp_path, tmp_path)

+        api.verify_paths(m, ['/stdlib', '/dynload', '/site'], '@canary',
+                         tmp_path, tmp_path, tmp_path, tmp_path)

+def test_ancestor_replacement_after_negative_observation_refuses(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    api, m, leaf = fixture_probe(tmp_path, monkeypatch)
+    real_stat = api._stat
+    def replace_after_absence(*args, **kwargs):
+        try:
+            return real_stat(*args, **kwargs)
+        except FileNotFoundError:
+            leaf.parent.rename(tmp_path / 'former-parent')
+            leaf.parent.mkdir()
+            raise
+    monkeypatch.setattr(api, '_stat', replace_after_absence)
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='BINDING_DRIFT'):
+        api.absent_leaf(m)

+        api.absent_leaf(m)

+def test_wrong_final_missing_name_is_not_approved(tmp_path, monkeypatch):

+        api.absent_leaf(m)

+@pytest.mark.parametrize('key,value', [('declared_absent_path', '/unknown/python314.zip'),
+                                     ('schema', 999), ('runtime_read_authority_expanded', True)])
+def test_malformed_or_changed_declaration_rejected(tmp_path, key, value):
+    import json

+    path = pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name('import-path-pins.json')
+    m = json.loads(path.read_bytes())

+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal):
+        api.load(tmp_path)

+def test_real_startup_observation_required_not_schema_template(tmp_path):
+    import json
+    api = module()
+    path = pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name('import-path-pins.json')
+    m = json.loads(path.read_bytes())
+    # The current ordinary pytest host does not have the exact admitted
+    # installed -I-B startup. No source/schema example supplies that observation.
+    if list(api.sys.path) == m['startup_expected']:
+        pytest.skip('run this negative in the separately admitted standalone source-test host')
+    with pytest.raises(api.ProbeRefusal, match='ACTUAL_ISOLATED_STARTUP'):
+        api.verify_startup(m)
